VI / WRITING SKILL

Informational Leaflet    النشرة المعلوماتية

- What is an informational leaflet?    ما هي النشرة المعلوماتية؟
- An informational leaflet is a printed or digital document designed to provide concise, factual, and easy-to-read information about a specific topic, product, service, or organization.
النشرة المعلوماتية هي وثيقة مطبوعة أو رقمية صُممت لتقديم معلومات موجزة، واضحة، وسهلة القراءة حول موضوع محدد، أو منتج أو خدمة، أو مؤسسة.

- What is an informational leaflet used for?    فيمَ تُستخدم النشرة المعلوماتية؟
- Its primary purpose is to educate the reader, raise awareness, or explain a process in a way that is accessible and quick to digest.
ويتّمثل هدفها الأساس في تثقيف القارئ، أو زيادة الوعي، أو شرح عملية ما بطريقة سهلة الفهم وسريعة الاستيعاب.

—— Main Parts of an Informational Leaflet    الأجزاء الرئيسية للنشرة المعلوماتية ——

1. Eye-catching headline    عنوان جذاب
Grabs the reader's attention immediately.    يجذب انتباه القارئ فوراً.
- Use a question or bold statement.    استخدم سؤالاً أو عبارة قوية وجريئة.
Useful Expressions:
- Why are you still…?    - Discover how…    - Ready to…?
e.g. Why are you still paying for energy the sun gives for free?

2. Subheadings    العناوين الجانبية
Organize the content into clear, easy-to-navigate sections.
نظّم المحتوى في أقسام واضحة يسهل الانتقال بينها.
Useful Expressions:
- What is…?    - Why should…?    - How do you…?
e.g. What is Solar Energy?

3. Bullet points    القوائم النقوطة
Present key information concisely and accessibly.
قم بعرض المعلومات الأساسية بإيجاز وبطريقة يسهل فهمها.
e.g. • Solar panels capture sunlight and convert it into electricity.
• Excess energy is stored in batteries.
• Young people find employment opportunities.

4. Call to action    الدعوة لاتخاذ إجراء
Encourages the reader to do something specific.
يشجع القارئ على القيام بإجراء محدد.
Useful Expressions:
• Switch to solar today.    • Talk to your school about …
• Visit … to learn more.    • Ready to make a difference?
e.g. Switch to Solar Today.

5, Tone ena Matches the intended audience-friendly yet informative for young people.
register يناسبان الجمهور المستهدف فيكون الأسلوب ودود ولكن غنى بالمعلومات ومناسبًا للشباب.
(friendly Useful Expressions:
tone) - Start small... - You can... - Talk to...

e.g. Start small: solar-powered chargers are a great first step. النبرة الودية
Images or diagrams support and enhance the written text.
تدعم الصور أو الرسوم التوضيحية النص المكتوب وتعزز محتواه. :
Visuals : .6
Useful Expressions: ا
: : : المؤثرات البصرية
pictures - icons - diagrams - charts -
e.g. solar panel icon / clean energy symbol.

◆ تدريبات Writing | Exercises On Writing

¢ Choose the correct answer from a, b, c or d:

1. Which sentence is most suitable for the short introduction of a leaflet about solar energy?
a. For example, solar panels can reduce electricity bills.
b. Talk to your family about installing solar panels today.
c. This guide explains how solar energy works and why it matters.
d. Solar panels capture sunlight and convert it into electricity.

2. Which subheading would best organize a section explaining the advantages of solar
a. Why Should You Choose Solar Power?
b. How Do Solar Panels Work?
c. What Is Solar Energy?
d. Where Can You Get More Information?

3. Which sentence uses a friendly but informative tone suitable for young readers?
a. Individuals are advised to initiate their engagement with limited solar-powered devices.
b. Start small—using a solar-powered charger is a great first step.
c.Solar-powered devices constitute a technically appropriate starting mechanism.
d.It is compulsory for all young people to purchase a solar-powered charger
immediately.
4. Which sentence is the clearest call to action at the end of a leaflet about clean transport?
a. Public transport can reduce the number of cars on busy roads.
b. Many cities now provide buses and trains for daily travel.
c. Try public transport this week and help reduce local pollution.
d. For example, one full bus can replace several private cars.

5. Which sentence uses a modal verb carefully to present a possible benefit rather than
a certain result?
a. Battery storage always solves every problem caused by solar power.
b. Battery storage will completely remove all interruptions in electricity supply.
c. Battery storage must solve the problem of changing energy production.

d. Battery storage may help provide electricity when sunlight is unavailable.

6. Which version uses the clearest language for an informational leaflet?
a. Solar panels transform solar radiation into usable electrical energy.
b. Solar panels convert incoming sunlight into domestic electrical power.

c. Solar panels turn sunlight into electricity for family homes.

d. Solar panels utilize sunlight to generate household electrical energy.

7. Which set uses brief action points to give practical advice about reducing food waste?
a. Food waste costs money. It wastes resources. It harms family budgets.
b. Why is food wasted? Who wastes it? What can families change?
c. Plan meals carefully. Buy only what you need. Store food safely.

d. Food is often wasted. Shops discard it. Families discard it too.

8. A leaflet has already explained how solar panels work. Which sentence logically begins
the next section on benefits?
a. Solar panels collect sunlight and convert it into electricity.
b. One key benefit is that solar power can lower bills.
c. For example, many homes receive strong sunlight throughout the year.

d. You can ask a trusted expert about suitable solar systems.

9. Which ending combines a balanced recommendation with a specific call to action?
a. Solar energy can reduce household bills in many suitable homes.
b. You can learn more about solar systems from this guide.
c. Families must install solar panels to solve every energy problem.

d. Consider your needs, then ask an expert about suitable options.

10. A leaflet contains accurate facts but is difficult to scan. Which revision improves
organization while using a visual to support the written text?
a. Add longer paragraphs and explain every point in greater detail.
b. Remove section headings and present all information as one block.
c. Use clear subheadings, bullet points, and one simple supporting diagram.

d. Replace most facts with colourful photographs and several short slogans.

a. increasing present production despite future damage
b. leaving useful resources completely unused for years
c. protecting future resources while ignoring current needs
d. meeting present needs without harming future generations

a. the willingness to work for several hours

b. the habit of completing tasks without assistance

c. the skill of performing several jobs simultaneously
d. the ability to work with minimum wasted resources

◆ الكتابة العملية | Writing in Action

Adverbials of Degree and Frequency

What is an adverb ? ما هو الظرف/الحال ؟
- It is a word that expresses frequency, time, place, purpose, degree or manner.
- الظرف هو كلمة تعبر عن التكرار أو الزمان أو المكان أو الغرض أو الدرجة أو الطريقة (الكيفية)، وي يمكن لتخص الظروف
في الخريطة الذهنية التالية:

- Outside
- Upstairs
- There
- Everywhere

- Often
- Never
- Always
- Sometimes

- Afterwards
- Before
- Still
- Later

Place | Frequency | Time

Adverbs

Manner | Purpose | Degree

- Quicklly
- Carefully
- Beautifully

- To avoid
- To get
- To remember

- Extremely

وقفيما يلي شرح مبسط للأنواع الظروف :

1 Adverbs of frequency : ظروف التكرار
تُستخدم ظروف التكرار للدلالة على درجة تكرار الحدث، وتستخدم ظروف التكرار التالية قبل جميع الأفعال ما عدا (be)
فَتَأتي بعده:

always دائماً – usually عادة – often/ frequently غالباً – sometimes أحياناً –
occasionally بين الحين والآخر – rarely/seldom نادراً – never أبداً –
consistently باستمرار – regularly بشكل منتظم …etc.

- Mr Nasser always arrives at work on time.
- Mr Nasser is always at work on time.

وأحياناً تَأتي بعض هذه الظروف في بداية أو نهاية الجملة :

- Sometimes, Yara prepares the family lunch.
- Yara prepares the family lunch sometimes.

وتَأتي ظروف التكرار التالية غالباً في بداية أو نهاية الجملة :

- once مرة - twice مرتين - three times - four times (an hour / a day /
a week / a month / a year …etc.)
- every (morning / day / week / year…)

- I get up early every morning. = Every morning, I get up early.

② Adverbs of time : ظروف الزمان

تُستخدم ظروف الزمان للدلالة على وقت حدوث الفعل، وغالباً تأتي في بداية أو نهاية الجملة وقد تأتي أثناءَ بعدَ الفعال المساعدة والناقصة، ومن أمثلة ظروف الزمان ما يلي:

today, yesterday, now, still, currently, afterwards, after, before, yet ... etc.

- I haven't done the shopping yet.
- I am sure we met somewhere before.
- She is still doing the shopping.

③ Adverbs of place : ظروف المكان

تُستخدم ظروف المكان للدلالة على مكان حدوث الفعل، وغالباً تأتي في بداية أو نهاية الجملة، ومن أمثلة ظروف الزمان ما يلي:

inside, outside, here, there, somewhere, everywhere, outdoors, indoors …etc.

- I have lived here since I was born.
- The children are playing outdoors.

④ Adverbs of purpose : ظروف الغرض

وهي عبارات ظرفية تبدأ بـ (to + inf.) بمعني (لي) وتستخدم للتعبير عن الغرض وتأتي في البداية أو النهاية :

- I follow a strict diet to lose weight.
- To overcome a challenge, you need to work hard.

⑤ Adverbs of degree : ظروف الدرجـة

تُستخدم ظروف الدرجـة لتحديد درجة الصفة، ومن أمثلة ظروف الدرجـة ما يلي :

quite/rather – إلى حد ما so – إلى حد كبير very-extremely – للغاية completely – utterly تماماً -
totally – كلياً absolutely – تماماً significantly – بشكل ملحوظ greatly – بدرجة كبيرة –
largely – إلى حد كبير considerably – بشكل ملحوظ slightly – قليلاً ...etc.

- The weather is very hot today.    - She is completely exhausted.

⑥ Adverbs of manner : ظروف الكيفية (الطريقة)

تُستخدم ظروف الكيفية لوصف كيفية حدوث الفعل وهي تمثل إجابة السؤال بـ (how) :

- A: How did Sama write ?    B: She wrote clearly.
- A: How does he walk ?    B: He walks quickly.

يأتي ظرف الكيفية بعد الفعل أو بعد المفعول:

- A tortoise moves slowly.
- Rodayna spoke to me quietly.

How to change an adjective into an adverb of manner :
طريقة تحويل الصفة إلى ظرف كيفية

١) الصفة عادة يتم تكوين ظرف الكيفية بإضافة (ly) إلى الصفة :
- impressive مُبهر – impressively بشكل مُبهر
- weak ضعيف – weakly بضعف
- significant ملحوظ – significantly بشكل ملحوظ
- quiet هادئ – quietly بهدوء

٢) وتُضاف (ly) حتى وإن كانت الصفة منتهية بحرف (l) :
- hopeful متفائل – hopefully من المُتشظر
- beautiful جميل – beautifully بطريقة جميلة

٣) الصفة المنتهية بـ (le) يتم حذف حرف (e) وإضافة (y) :
- terrible فظيع – terribly بشكل فظيع
- sensible عقلاني – sensibly بعقلانية

٤) الصفة المنتهية بـ (y) مسبوق بحرف ساكن يتحول إلى (ily) :
- happy سعيد – happily بسعادة
- crazy مجنون – crazily بجنون

٥) الصفة المنتهية بـ (ly) تحول إلى ظرف باستخدام (in a ........ way) :
- friendly ودود – in a friendly way بود/بحميمية
- cowardly جبان – in a cowardly way بجُبن

٦) هناك ظروف غير منتظمة مثل:
- good جيد – well جيدا          - fast سريع – fast بسرعة
- hard جاد – hard بجد          - late مُتأخّر – late مُتأخّر
- early مُبكّر – early مُبكّرا

لُحظ الفرق : Clear up the confusion :

(1) hard / hardly

• hard (adj)          صلب - ضعب - شاق (صفة)
- This rock is too hard صلب to be broken easily.
- Last year's exam was really hard . صعب
- He got tired of the hard شاق work in that factory.

• hard (adv)          بجد - بقوة (ظرف)
- My father has worked hard to provide a good life for our family.
- You must hit the rock hard to break it.

• hardly ... (any / at all) (adv)          بالتّكد / قلما
- I have eaten hardly anything today.
- She hardly meets her old friends at all.

* hardly ever = almost never علي التفي) Jai) بالْكاد / قلما

- She hardly ever goes out = she almost never goes out.
- He hardly speaks Spanish, does he ?

@ late / lately

• 1216 - later - latest (adj)
بمعني (متأخر) - أي أنها تأتي قبل الاسم أو بعد الأفعال التقريرية: (adjective) كصفة (late) تُستخدم
- I am late for school.
- The head teacher punished the late students.
: بمعني (أواخر/قرب النهاية) - وتأتي قبل الاسم فقط (adjective) كصفة (late) تستخدم
- She is in her late forties.

• late - later — (no superlative) (adv)
: (Stic) يمعني (adverb) كظرف (late) تُستخدم
- I am late for school.
: (a2) Loss) كظرف بمعني (later) تستخدم
- I will call you later.
: بمعني (مُؤخْراً/حديثاً) (adverb) «4,45 (lately) تستخدم
- [have visited my aunt lately. (= recently)
: (latter) و (later) الفرق بين Bow

° later (adv) بعد los = after some time
- 1 met her two years later. (= after two years)

* later (adj) JU - dou
- We will discuss this problem in a later meeting.
° latter (n) الآخر - الثاني
- [have two daughters: Rodayna and Sama. The latter is younger.
- We will announce the result of the competition in the latter part of the program.

© most / most of / mostly

: بمعني (غالبية /مفعظم/جُل): (adjective) كصفة (most/most of) primi
- Most students like Mr Nasser. Ee more than 50% of the students)
- Most of my friends go to the same school. (= more than 50% of my friends)
بمعني (الأكثر): (adverb) كظرف (most) تُستخدم
- Amira is the most intelligent student in class.
- 1 play tennis and handball, but I like football most/the most.
بمعني (أساساً/عامةً/عادةً): (adverb) كظرف (mostly) تستخدم

- We mostly play football, but sometimes we play table tennis.

4 good / well
كلمة (good) صفة بينما كلمة (well) ظرف:

Adjective / adverb | Comparative | Superlative
good(adj)  جيد - صالح - مفيد | better  أفضل | best  الأفضل
well(adj)  جيد - صالح - مفيد | better  بشكل أفضل | best  بشكل الأفضل

تُستخدم (good) صفة (adjective) بمعنى (جيد/صالح/طيب):
- Mr Taha is a good man.
- His good presentation impressed us all.
تُستخدم (well) ظرف (adverb) بمعنى (جيدا/بشكل جيد):
- She did well in the final exam.
تُستخدم (well) صفة (adjective) بمعنى (بصحة جيدة/على ما يرام):
- A: How are you? B: Very well, thanks.
يمكن استخدام (good) كإسم (noun) بمعنى الخير:
- Exercise does me good.

Important notes on adverbs  ملاحظات هامة على الظروف
1 الصفة تصف إسم بينما ظرف الكيفية يصف فعل :
... verb فعل + adverb ظرف / adjective صفة + noun
- She is a good speaker. / She speaks well.
- Amir is a quick runner. / Amir runs quickly.
- Chess is an indoor game. / Chess is played indoors.
2 بعد أداة الاستفهام (how) نستخدم صفة لوصف إسم ونستخدم ظرف لوصف فعل :
- How good is his behaviour?
- How well does he behave?
3 يمكن مقارنة الظروف مثل الصفات الطويلة بإستخدام :
more أكثر / less أقل + adv. الظرف + than
- Sama writes more carefully than Ahmed.
- I drive less quickly than Moataz.
4 تأتي ظروف الشك /الاحتمال او التكرار قبل الفعل وليس بعده والعكس مع (be) :
usually – probably – possibly – definitely .........
- It is possible that he will visit us. = - He will possibly visit us.
5 هناك ظروف يمكن أن تُستخدم لتغير معنى الجملة بالكامل وتُسمى (Sentence Adverbs), وغالبا تستخدم في بداية الجملة مثل:
Fortunately, / Luckily, لحسن الحظ — unfortunately, /Unluckily, لسوء الحظ — Strangely, بوضوح clearly, من المدهش — Surprisingly, من المثير للاهتمام — Interestingly, من الغريب — undoubtedly, بلا شك — arguably, من الممكن أن — currently, حاليا — generally, بوجه عام ...
- Luckily, I found my lost keys.
- Strangely, he agreed to help us.

(Notes for application =)

© ... verb Jos + adverb ظرف =... adjective صفة + in/ at... + (inf. + ing)
- She speaks English well. = She is good at speaking English.

© ... adverb ظرف =in + adj. صفة + way / manner.

- His behaviour was friendly. = He behaved in a friendly way.

© It is+adj.aao = adverb ظرف

- It is strange that she refused the offer.
= Strangely, she refused the offer.

© ... be + adverb ظرف + adj. صفة = have + adj. صفة +noun.......
- lam seriously ill. = I have a serious illness.

Subject + never / rarely / hardly / no sooner ... + verb .......
= Never / Rarely / Hardly / No sooner/Little + مساعد Jos + subj. + verb...

- Mai never went shopping.
_=Rarely did Mai go shopping.

Adverbials in Argumentative Writing

Use adverbials of degree and frequency to make your ideas about Egypt’s environmental
progress more precise and persuasive.

Quick Writitng Tips
نصائح سريعة للكتابة
• Degree adverbials strengthen claims.
ظروف الدرجة تقوى الفكرة أو الرأي.
• Frequency adverbials describe regular progress or repeated action. ظروف التكرار توضح التقدم المستمر أو الفعل المتكرر.
• Stance adverbials show the writer's opinion.
ظروف الموقف تُظهر رأي الكاتب.

◆ مهمة الكتابة | Writing Task
مهمة كتابية
• Write an argumentative paragraph (150-180 words) about Egypt's green future. Use adverbials of degree to strengthen your ideas and adverbials of frequency to show how environmental efforts are developing.
اكتب فقرة جدلية من ١٥٠ إلى ١٨٠ كلمة عن مستقبل مصر الأخضر. استخدم ظروف الدرجة لتقوية أفكارك، وظروف التكرار لتوضيح كيف تتطور الجهود البيئية.

Checklist
قائمة المراجعة
1. At least two real examples of green projects in Egypt.
مثالان حقيقيان على الأقل لمشروعات خضراء في مصر.
2. Your own suggestions for improvement.
مقترحاتك الخاصة للتطوير.
3. At least one cause-and-effect structure.
تركيب واحد على الأقل للسبب والنتيجة.
4. A clear concluding sentence.
جملة ختامية واضحة.
5. At least two adverbials of degree.
ظرفا درجة على الأقل.
6. At least two adverbials of frequency.
ظرفا تكرار على الأقل.

affordable (adj) ميسور التكلفة

Goes with:
• affordable technology تكنولوجيا ميسورة التكلفة
• affordable solution حل مُتاح

Word Family

Word / Example
afford (v) يقدر / يتحمل التكلفة — Many families can afford simple green technology.
affordable (adj) ميسور التكلفة — The solar water heater was affordable for lower-income families.
affordability (n) القدرة على تحمل التكلفة — Affordability is important for green solutions.

©9 Don’t mix

٠ affordable = not too expensive ميسور التكلفة
- Solar panels are becoming more affordable every year.
٠ available = ready or possible to get متاح
- Free training courses are available for young innovators.

Find the word or phrase in the text that could meaningfully replace the words below.

The climate report warned that the sea was changing because of growing acidity. Coral
reefs also suffered from whitening after weeks of warm water. Experts showed agreement
that the problem was serious. They said that the biological diversity of the reef was
decreasing, while storms were gaining strength. Even a small portion of a degree could make
the situation worse. Because of this, coastal communities need urgent plans to protect homes,
farms and natural habitats before the damage becomes harder to control.

Word / Phrase

Answer from the text

1. acidification
2. biodiversity
3. bleaching

4. consensus
5. fraction

6. intensity
